```
def _lbfgs_optimize(a_init, deadline, min_remaining=60, beta_scale=None, max_iter_cap=...):
    n=len(a_init)
    c=_fft_convolve(a_init,a_init)
    M=float(c.max())
    sorted_c=np.sort(c)[::-1]
    gap = sorted_c[0] - sorted_c[min(10,len-1)] if len>10 else sorted_c[0]
    base_beta = max(gap, M*0.02, 1e-12)
    if n>=16384: base_beta maybe M*0.01? Tune.
    betas = base_beta * np.array([...])
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a_init,1e-30)); z-=z.mean()
    current_a=a_init.copy(); current_sc=_score_raw(current_a)
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       if time.monotonic() + min_remaining > deadline: break
       remaining=deadline-min_remaining-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 10: break
       # estimate max_iter: maybe for n<=8192 0.005s*8? We'll set based on n
       est = 0.01 * max(1.0, n/4096.0)  # per function eval? For n=8192 ~0.02? Actually c+grad ~0.03? L-BFGS iter maybe 0.1. Use 0.1* n/8192.
       max_iter = min(5000, max(100, int(remaining/(0.15*max(1,n/8192.0)))))
       # But scipy each iter multiple eval; use conservative.
       _OBJ_N=n; _OBJ_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res=minimize(_lsobj, z, jac=_lsgrad, method='L-BFGS', options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxcor':50,'maxls':50})
          a_new=_softmax(res.x - np.mean(res.x))
          sc=_score_raw(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_sc - 1e-14:
             current_a=a_new; current_sc=sc; z=np.log(np.maximum(a_new,1e-30)); z-=z.mean()
       except Exception:
          pass
    return current_a, current_sc
```

```
def _weighted_newton(a_init, deadline, max_iter=100, top_tol=0.002, soft_beta=None, max_cg=60):
    n=len(a_init)
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_raw(a)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c=_fft_convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max()); sc=2.0*n*M/(a.sum()**2?) a sum1 -> 2nM.
       if not finite break
       if M < 1e-300: break
       if soft_beta is None:
           # top uniform
           tol=top_tol*M
           active = c >= (M - tol)
           if active.sum()==0: active[argmax]=True
           # cap top 3n? Actually length 2n-1. If too many, take top max(1, n) perhaps.
           if active.sum() > 1.5*n:
               active = np.zeros_like(c, bool)
               idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max(1,int(1.5*n))]
               active[idx]=True
           w=active.astype(float); w/=w.sum()
       else:
           # softmax over all with beta_soft relative M
           beta_s = soft_beta * M if soft_beta<1 else soft_beta
           shift=(c-M)/beta_s
           e=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-700,0)); w=e/e.sum()
           # optionally zero tiny weights if too many? maybe not.
       h=_corr_a_A(a,w)
       g=h - np.mean(h)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       # HVP matvec with w
       mu = max(1e-9 / n? , 1e-12? )
```

Actually w sum1, hankel entries average diagonal. Matrix may have small norm O(1). If v~1/n, Hv~1/n^2? So mu=1e-8 may dominate for n=8192? gnorm~1/n=1e-4, H v~1e-8, mu v~1e-12, okay. CG:
```
       x=np.zeros(n); r=g - hvp(x); p=r.copy(); rs=np.dot(r,r)
       for cg in range(max_cg):
           Ap=hvp(p)
           pAp=np.dot(p,Ap)
           if pAp <= 1e-30: break
           alpha=rs/pAp
           xnew=x+alpha*p
           rnew=r-alpha*Ap
           rsnew=np.dot(rnew,rnew)
           if np.sqrt(rsnew) < 1e-10 * max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(g)): break
           p=rnew + (rsnew/rs)*p
           r=rnew; x=xnew; rs=rsnew
       # If CG failed (rs not finite), x=zeros.
```

Let's decide final program structure with focus on robustness and improvement:

Multiplicative KKT projection could be new and useful. Let's formalize a robust update:
Given a sum1. Choose target weights w over convolution lags based on current c and a target M_target (or upper bound). We want reduce c. A natural potential: Φ = ∑_k D_k where D_k = c_k - M if c_k>M. For each coefficient, derivative =2h_i. Mirror descent update log a_i -= η * a_i? Not simple. But a simple multiplicative water-filling:
- Compute `h_i = corr(a, w)`, where w_k = max(c_k - target, 0) maybe normalized. If h_i high, coefficient contributes a lot to above-target lags. Update a_i <- a_i * exp(-η h_i) (or divide by (1+η h_i)). Normalize. This reduces high h. Then c changes. Repeat. This is like penalizing high-weight convolutions.
- Choose w_k = max(c_k - M_target,0) / total. M_target = current_best_amps * (1 - margin). This directly reduces violations. As a changes, update.
This can be very fast. Let's call it multiplicative penalty (MP).

Multiplicative shave details:
```
def _multiplicative_shave(a_init, deadline, max_iter=2000, target_frac=0.9995, step_sizes=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0), mix=0.002):
    a = normalize(a_init)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = score(a)
    n = len(a)
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        c = _fft_convolve(a,a)
        M = float(c.max())
        sc = 2*n*M  # since sum1
        # target peak = max(best_sc_for_current_n? We can pass best_score global? Use best_sc * target_frac maybe)
        target_peak = best_sc / (2.0*n) * target_frac  # current best score for this n (not global? use local best)
        # But if target below min c maybe no violations; then use M*0.999?
        target_peak = max(target_peak, M * 0.999 - 1e-30, 0.0)??
```

        if weights sum small (target near max only one), use all lags with weights proportional c/M? To avoid zero.
        w = np.clip(c - target_peak, 0.0); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<1e-15: w = c/M; wsum=1.
        w /= wsum
        h = _corr_a_A(a,w)
        # choose step that improves score
        cur_sc = 2*n*M
        best_step = 0.0; best_try = cur_sc
        for step in step_sizes:
            # multiplicative update: a_try = a / (1 + step*h) ? If h up to maybe 10/n? For uniform w, h~? step=1 small.
            # Use log update: a_try = a * np.exp(-step * (h - h.mean()))? Since sum a should ~1; subtract mean to keep roughly. But penalty weights nonnegative; h - mean can be negative, increasing some coefficients.
            # KKT wants h constant; we can do a_try = a * exp(-step*(h - hmean))
            hmean = float(np.mean(h))
            z_update = -step * (h - hmean)
            # Clip to avoid overflow
            z_update = np.clip(z_update, -3, 3)
            a_try = a * np.exp(z_update)
            # mix with uniform to keep positive
            if mix>0: a_try = (1-mix)*a_try + mix*(1.0/n)
            a_try = normalize(a_try)
            c_try = _fft_convolve(a_try,a_try); sc_try=2*n*c_try.max()
            if sc_try < best_sc_local - 1e-15:
                best_sc_local=sc_try; best_a=a_try; best_step=step
        if best_step==0:
            # maybe try smaller steps 1e-3,1e-4
            for step in (0.005,0.001,5e-4): ...
            if no improve: break? or random restart from best with perturb.
        a = best_a
        if best_sc_local < best_sc: best_a=...
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Weighted Newton robust using Richardson:
```
def _newton_direction(a,w, max_iter=10):
    g = h - mean(h)
    # compute H g
    Hg = _corr_a_A(g,w)
    # maybe Hg should be tangent? subtract mean?
    Hg -= mean(Hg)
    dh = dot(g,Hg)
    if dh <= 0: return -g  # fallback
    # Richardson for H u = g. Starting u=0.
    beta = 0.5 * dot(g,g)/dh
    u = np.zeros_like(g)
    for k in range(max_iter):
        res = g - _corr_a_A(u,w)
        res -= mean(res)
        u += beta*res
        # maybe recompute beta if needed
    return u
```
But H u includes no factor? HVP = T_w u. We want solve T u = g (or 2T). Since g = h - mean. Direction for descent is -u (since F quadratic Hessian 2T, Newton d=-H^{-1}g). If T indefinite, `dh` could be negative. If positive, Richardson may work. We can line search on actual score for step t in [1,0.5,...] for a+t*d. If no improvement, try small step. This may be faster than CG and robust. We can also use a few Lanczos vectors to approximate inverse: Build Krylov V with g, Hg, H^2g, solve least squares. - Build basis Q via Lanczos without full reorth (or with) to approximate H. Then solve projected system. This may handle indefinite by using eigendecomposition (since symmetric). If we have orthonormal basis Q (n×K), H ≃ Q T Q^T + (I-QQ^T) ignoring. We want solve H u = g. Project into basis: z = Q^T g. Solve T z_new = z, with regularization for small eigenvalues. Then u=Q z_new. If H indefinite, eigenvalues can be negative; regularize dividing by eigenvalues if |λ|>tol. This is a spectral Newton step. It can be robust and fast if K=30-60. HVP cost K. def _hankel_hvp(v, w):
```
```
   hv = _corr_a_A(v,w)
   hv = hv - np.mean(hv)
   return hv
```
But in `_krylov_newton_direction`, need many calls; calling `_corr_a_A` allocates. okay.

Lanczos reorthogonalization: To maintain orthonormality, for K=30, without reorth may lose stability. We can do full reorth each step: after new q, subtract projections onto all previous V. Cost additional dot products K per step, K^2 dots length n. For n=65536, K=30, K^2 n ~57M operations, okay maybe 0.05s. We can implement for robustness. Or use Arnoldi with reorth.
```
V=[]; q=g/gnorm
for j in range(K):
   V.append(q)
   Hp=_hvp(q,w)
   # orthogonalize against all V
   for v in V:
      Hp -= np.dot(q,v)? Wait for new q after constructing, need alpha for current.
```
Standard:
For j current q (orthonormal to previous):
   Hp = H q
   alpha = dot(q, Hp)
   Hp = Hp - alpha*q
   for l in range(j): Hp -= dot(V[l], Hp)*V[l]
   beta=norm(Hp)
   if beta small break
   q=Hp/beta
   for l in range(j+1): q -= dot(V[l], q)*V[l]

Set betas[j]=beta.
```

Build T = diag(alpha) + offdiag(beta). Solve `/eigh`.

Implementation careful:
```
def _hankel_hvp_proj(v, w):
    hv = _corr_a_A(v, w)
    hv -= np.mean(hv)
    return hv

def _krylov_direction(g, w, K=30, drop_tol=1e-7):
    n=len(g)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    g -= np.mean(g)
    gnorm = np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gnorm < 1e-300: return np.zeros_like(g)
    V=[]
    q = g / gnorm
    alphas=[]; betas=[]
    for j in range(K):
        V.append(q)
        Hp = _hankel_hvp_proj(q, w)
        alpha = float(np.dot(q, Hp))
        alphas.append(alpha)
        Hp = Hp - alpha * q
        # reorthogonalize
        for l in range(len(V)-1):
            vprev = V[l]
            Hp = Hp - float(np.dot(vprev, Hp)) * vprev
        beta = float(np.linalg.norm(Hp))
        if beta < 1e-12 * max(1.0, gnorm):
            break
        q = Hp / beta
        # reorth q
        for l in range(len(V)):
            vprev = V[l]
            q = q - float(np.dot(vprev, q)) * vprev
        qn = float(np.linalg.norm(q))
        if qn < 1e-12: break
        q = q / qn
        betas.append(beta)
    m = len(V)
    # build T
    T = np.diag(alphas[:m])
    for i in range(min(m-1, len(betas))):
        T[i,i+1]=betas[i]; T[i+1,i]=betas[i]
    # right-hand side norm e0
    rhs = np.zeros(m); rhs[0]=gnorm
    try:
       vals, vecs = np.linalg.eigh(T)
    except:
       return -g
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(vals))) if m>0 else 1.0
    tol = drop_tol * max(scale, 1e-15)
    inv = np.zeros(m)
    # Maybe if positive eigenvalues small but negative significant? drop small.
    for i, val in enumerate(vals):
        if abs(val) > tol:
            inv[i] = 1.0 / val
        else:
            inv[i] = 0.0
    y = vecs.T @ (vecs @ rhs * inv)  # check shapes: (m,m).T @ (m,)
    u = np.zeros(n)
    for i, v in enumerate(V):
        if y[i] != 0.0:
            u += y[i] * v
    # Ensure tangent
    u -= np.mean(u)
    # If direction not descent? Check dot with g; Newton direction should -u. We return -u.
    d = -u
    # If d dot g >=0, maybe sign issue; return -g
    if float(np.dot(d, g)) >= -1e-12*gnorm*gnorm:
        d = -g
        d -= np.mean(d)
    return d
```
Check rhs orientation: In Lanczos, T is m×m, Q matrix columns V. H Q = Q T (plus). We want solve H u = g. Let u=Q y. Then H u ≈ Q T y. Need Q T y = g = gnorm Q0. Project onto Q: T y = gnorm e0. Good. `vecs` columns eigenvectors. y = Vmat diag(inv) Vmat.T rhs.
If H indefinite, exact solve may produce u. Direction -u. Descent condition for quadratic with Hessian T: d^T g = -u^T g. u^T g =? If H SPD, u solves H u=g, u^T g >0, descent. If indefinite, may not. Check.

Line search in spectral Newton:
```
def _spectral_newton(a_init, deadline, max_iter=20, top_tol=0.002, K=30, beta_w_factor=None):
  a=normalize
  best...
  for it:
    c=conv; M=max; sc=2nM
    if soft:
       beta_w = (top_tol if beta_w_factor is None else beta_w_factor)*M
       shift=(c-M)/beta_w; e=exp; w=e/e.sum()
    else:
       active = c >= M - top_tol*M; ... w uniform active
    h=corr(a,w); g=h-mean(h); gnorm=norm(g)
    if gnorm < 1e-14: break
    d=_krylov_direction(g,w,K)
    # If d norm 0 fallback -g
    # tmax
    neg=d< -1e-14
    if neg.any(): tmax=0.99*np.min(a[neg]/(-d[neg]))
    else: tmax=1.0
    t=min(1.0, tmax)
    accepted=False
    for ls in range(40):
       a_try=a + t*d
       if min< -1e-12: t*=0.5 continue
       a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0); normalize
       c_try=conv; sc_try=2n max
       if sc_try < sc - 1e-13*t*gnorm or ls==39 and sc_try < sc:
          a=a_try; best update; accepted=True; break
       t*=0.5
    if not accepted:
       # fallback gradient
       d=-g; ...
       if not accepted: break
    # convergence
```
We need factor: g is gradient of weighted sum (without 4n). The score difference scale. Armijo enough.
```

```
Multiplicative update: Let's refine to be more effective. We can define several update templates:
```
def _multiplicative_shave(a, deadline, max_iter, mode='penalty', target_frac=0.999, step_sizes=...):
```
For each iter:
- c, M.
- If mode penalty: target = M * target_frac. w = clip(c-target,0). If w.sum < 1e-12: w = c/M normalized.
- h=corr(a,w); h_centered = h - mean(h).
- For step in step_sizes: u = a * exp(-step * h_centered); mix small; normalize; evaluate.
- Choose best step. If none, try with `h` not centered? If all c at target, no improve break.
- To avoid stagnation, periodically add small random perturbation if no improve for 50 iters.
This can run many iterations. Use `deadline` check.

Step sizes: h_centered scale ~1/n (1e-4 to 1e-5). exp(-step*h) ~1 for step 1. Need larger step to move significantly. If h ~1e-4, step=100 gives exp(-0.01) small change. Score improvements tiny. Maybe step sizes should scale with n: `base = 1.0 / mean(a) = n`. h~? For uniform w, h_i = ∑ w_k a_{k-i}; w sum1, a sum1, h ~1/n. So step*n ~ O1. Use step sizes: [0.1,1,10,100,1000,5000] times? More generally choose `scale = 1.0 / max(np.mean(a), 1e-15)` = n. Try `step_scale * scale` for factors [0.1,0.5,1,5,20,100]. This gives exponent O(0.1..100). Let's write `_lsobj` stable:
```
def _lsobj(z):
    n=_OBJ_N; beta=max(1e-12, _OBJ_BETA)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _fft_convolve(a,a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    shift = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
    S = float(np.sum(e))
    if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        val = 2.0 * n * cmax
    else:
        val = 2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * float(np.log(S)))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 2.0 * n * cmax
    return float(val)
```
If cmax maybe 0, beta. okay.
`_lsgrad` similar. `h = _corr_a_A(a, w)`. `W = float(np.dot(w, c))`. `grad = 4.0*n*a*(h - W)`. Sum zero? subtract mean. If not finite, return zeros.

Potential factor: If using objective O=2n*(cmax + beta logS), gradient wrt z =? We derived 4n a(h-W) where W=∑w c. Good. No beta. So gradient independent of beta except weights. This means as beta→0, gradient tends to gradient of max times scale. Good.

```
def _multiplicative_shave(a_init, deadline, max_iter=2000, target_frac=0.998, min_iter=0, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = len(a)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = _score_raw(a)
    stale=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c=_fft_convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max()); sc=float(2.0*n*M) # a sum1 but use score raw
       if not finite: break
       # target: reduce below best_sc for this n? use max(current_sc*target_frac, best_sc*target_frac)
       target = max(current_M * target_frac, best_sc/(2.0*n) * target_frac)
       target = min(target, M)
       w = np.clip(c - target, 0.0)
       wsum = w.sum()
       if wsum < 1e-14 * M or wsum <=0:
           # if no violation after clipping, use top active weights (c near M)
           top_tol = 0.001*M
           w = np.where(c >= M - top_tol, 1.0, 0.0)
           wsum = w.sum()
           if wsum ==0: w[np.argmax(c)]=1; wsum=1
       w = w / wsum
       h = _corr_a_A(a,w)
       hmean = float(np.mean(h))
       hspan = float(np.max(h) - np.min(h))
       if hspan < 1e-15:
           # try tiny random if breath?
           if rng is not None and it%50==0:
              a = _normalize(a + 1e-4*rng.standard_normal(n))
           stale += 1
           if stale > 30: break
           continue
       stale=0
       candidates = [0.2/hspan, 1.0/hspan, 5.0/hspan, 20.0/hspan, 100.0/hspan]
       # maybe if hspan large, cap
       best_step=0; best_try_sc=sc; best_try_a=None
       for s in candidates:
           zup = -s * (h - hmean)
           zup = np.clip(zup, -20.0, 20.0)
           at = a * np.exp(zup)
           at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
           # add small floor
           at = at + 1e-8 / n
           at = _normalize(at)
           ct = _fft_convolve(at, at)
           atsc = float(2.0*n*ct.max())
           if atsc < best_try_sc - 1e-14:
               best_try_sc=atsc; best_try_a=at; best_step=s
       if best_try_a is not None:
           a=best_try_a; sc=best_try_sc
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
               best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
           # try very small steps
           small = [0.01/hspan, 0.05/hspan]
           ...
           if none:
              # perturb
              if rng is not None:
                 a = _normalize(a + 1e-3*rng.standard_normal(n))
              stale +=1
              if stale > 40: break
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Using `sc=2nM` only if a sum1. We normalize. Good.
This update can be used as pre-polish. It may not beat L-BFGS but can.

Spectral Newton function:
```
def _spectral_newton(a_init, deadline, max_iter=30, weight_tol=0.005, K=None, max_ls=40):
    a=_normalize(a_init); n=len(a)
    if K is None: ...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_raw(a)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       c=conv; M=max; sc=score
       beta_w = max(1e-12, weight_tol*M)
       shift=(c-M)/beta_w; e=exp...; w=e/e.sum()
       # optionally if effective active small? okay
       h=corr(a,w); g=h-mean; gnorm=norm; if gnorm<1e-14 break
       d=_krylov_direction(g,w,K)
       dnorm=norm(d)
       if dnorm<1e-14: d=-g; d-=mean; dnorm=norm
       # if direction not descent, d=-g
       if dot(d,g) > -1e-14*gnorm:
           d=-g; d-=mean
       # tmax
       neg = d < -1e-14
       if neg.any(): tmax = 0.99*np.min(a[neg]/(-d[neg]))
       else: tmax = 1.0
       t = min(1.0, tmax)
       accepted = False
       for ls in range(max_ls):
           at = a + t*d
           if np.min(at) < -1e-10:
               t *= 0.5; continue
           at = np.maximum(at,0); at = _normalize(at)
           ct=conv; ats=float(2*n*ct.max())
           if ats < sc - 1e-9*t*max(1.0,gnorm) or (ls==max_ls-1 and ats < sc - 1e-13):
               a=at; sc=ats; accepted=True; break
           t *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           # fallback gradient step
           d = -g; d-=mean; normalize
           t...
           if not accepted: break
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       # convergence if max step small?
       if dnorm < 1e-12 * n? break
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Need ensure `_score_raw` returns 2nM/s^2 but normalized sum1. okay.

Eigh T m×m, m<=K. Good.
```

```
Pseudo code complete:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OBJ_N=0
_OBJ_BETA=1.0

def _next_pow2(n):
    k=1
    while k < n:
        k <<=1
    return 2 if k<2 else k

def _fft_convolve(u,v):
    u=np.asarray(u,float); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(u); m=len(v); out=n+m-1
    if out<=0: return np.empty(0)
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    fu=np.fft.rfft(u,N)
    fv=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fu*fv,N)[:out]
    return r
```
Could optimize for u==v by one rfft? `_fft_convolve(a,a)` currently two rfft. We can add `_fft_autocorr(a)` computes rfft once. But `_fft_convolve` used many with a,a. Let's implement separate `_auto_convolve(a)`:
```
def _auto_conv(a):
   n=len(a); out=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(out); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:out]
```
Use for c. For `_corr_a_A`, need different. This saves time. In score and updates use `_auto_conv`. In `_fft_convolve` only general maybe not needed. We can keep.

`_corr_a_A`:
```
def _corr_a_A(a,A):
    n=len(a); m=len(A); out=n+m-1; N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N); fA=np.fft.rfft(A,N); r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fA,N)[:out]
    return r[n-1: n+n-1]  # length n
```

```
def _score_raw(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return np.inf
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e12)??
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-15: return np.inf
   c=_auto_conv(a)
   m=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*len(a)*m/(s*s))
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s > 1e-15:
      a=a/s
   else:
      a=np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size) if a.size else np.array([1.0])
   return a
```

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=z-np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
    return e/s
```
Since z mean zero, max maybe. Good.

`_interpolate_to_n`:
```
def _interpolate_to_n(a,n_new):
   a=_normalize(a); n=len(a)
   if n==n_new: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
   a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return _normalize(a_new)

def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_remaining=30.0):
    global _OBJ_N, _OBJ_BETA
    a=_normalize(a_init); n=len(a)
    current_sc=_score_raw(a)
    c=_auto_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300: return a, current_sc
    cs=np.sort(c)[::-1]
    gap = cs[0] - cs[min(9,len(cs)-1)] if len(cs)>10 else cs[0]
    base = max(gap, M*0.02, M*1e-6, 1e-15)
    if n <= 4096:
       factors=[1.0,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.16,0.1,0.06,0.04,0.025,0.015,0.009,0.005,0.003,0.0018,0.001,0.0006,0.00035,0.0002,0.00012,0.00007,0.00004]

          pass

"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OBJ_N = 0
_OBJ_BETA = 1.0

def _next_pow2(m):
    k = 1
    while k < m:
        k <<= 1
    return 2 if k < 2 else k
```
If m=1, k=1, return2. okay.

`_auto_conv(a)` maybe use rfft of a; for real arrays, `fa*fa` not conjugate? For autocorrelation, need fa * np.conj(fa). Since FFT of real, multiplication by itself (not conjugate) computes circular autocorrelation? Wait! Critical. Circular convolution over complex: If we want linear convolution a*a, FFT: IFFT(FFT(a) * FFT(a)). That is product, not conjugate. Yes because convolution not correlation. For autocorrelation (a rev * a) would use conj. Here convolution a*a (linear) product same. Good. If a real, product okay. For squared magnitude? No.

`_corr_a_A(a,A)`: We need compute h_i=∑_p a_p A_{i+p}. This is cross-correlation-like. Using `a[::-1]` convolved with A: result[n-1+i] =∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a[n-1-j] A[n-1+i-j]. Let p=n-1-j, gives a[p] A[i+p]. Good. In FFT, convolution of reversed a and A. No conjugate. Good.

`_score_raw`: Use `_auto_conv`. For nonnegative, max positive. If c has tiny negative due roundoff, max positive.
```

```
`_make_profile`: For `kind='fourier_random'`, if rng provided. We'll implement simple:
```
def _make_profile(n, kind, rng=None):
   if kind=='uniform': return np.ones(n)/n
   if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if kind=='sin': a=np.sin(np.pi*t); ...
   if kind=='cos': a=1+0.4*np.cos(2πk t) ...
   if kind=='asym': a=np.exp(-3.0*((t-0.3)**2)/0.1) ...
   if kind=='power': a=(t+1e-6)**2 ...
   ...

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    parent = None
    n_base = 0
    try:
       parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if parent_raw.size > 0:
          parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
          s = float(parent_raw.sum())
          if s > 0.01:
             parent = parent_raw / s
             n_base = len(parent)
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    if parent is None:
       n_base = 4096
       parent = _make_profile(n_base, 'uniform', rng)
    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_sc = _score_raw(best_a)
    print(f"init n={len(best_a)} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # If no parent and uniform score high, maybe quick optimize at small n? targets cover.

    # targets up to 32768
    max_base = 32768
    targets=[]
    cur = max(256, n_base)
    while len(targets) < 4 and cur <= max_base:
       targets.append(cur)
       if cur >= max_base: break
       cur *= 2
    # deduplicate
    uniq=[]
    for x in targets:
       if x not in uniq: uniq.append(x)
    targets=uniq

    final_reserve = 250.0 if len(targets)>=3 else 120.0
    main_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + max(600.0, (deadline - t_start) - final_reserve))
    total_alloc = max(300.0, main_deadline - t_start)
    weights = [float(i+1) for i in range(len(targets))]
    sw = sum(weights)
    cursor = time.monotonic()
    prev_best_sc = best_sc
    improved_at_last = False
    last_target_sc = best_sc
    for idx, n_target in enumerate(targets):
       if time.monotonic() > main_deadline - 100.0: break
       alloc = total_alloc * weights[idx] / sw
       # Ensure not overrun; if alloc too small skip
       t_now = time.monotonic()
       if t_now + min(120.0, alloc) > main_deadline - 80.0:
           # use whatever remaining but at least 80
           if main_deadline - t_now < 100: break
           alloc = main_deadline - t_now - 80
           if alloc < 80: continue
       size_deadline = min(main_deadline - 30.0, t_now + alloc)
       budget = size_deadline - t_now
       if budget < 80: continue
       a_init = _interpolate_to_n(best_a, n_target)
       size_best_a = a_init
       size_best_sc = _score_raw(size_best_a)
       # LBFGS
       lb_budget = min(0.55 * budget, budget - 40)
       lb_deadline = min(size_deadline - max(40, 0.4*budget), t_now + lb_budget)
       if lb_deadline - time.monotonic() > 30:
           a_lb, sc_lb = _run_lbfgs(a_init, lb_deadline, min_remaining=10.0)
           if np.isfinite(sc_lb) and sc_lb < size_best_sc:
               size_best_a, size_best_sc = a_lb, sc_lb
       # multiplicative
       mp_budget = 0.22 * budget
       t_now2 = time.monotonic()
       mp_deadline = min(size_deadline - max(30, 0.25*budget), t_now2 + mp_budget)
       if mp_deadline - t_now2 > 20:
           a_mp, sc_mp = _multiplicative_shave(size_best_a, mp_deadline, rng=rng)
           if np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp < size_best_sc:
               size_best_a, size_best_sc = a_mp, sc_mp
       # spectral
       sp_deadline = min(size_deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + (budget - 0.8*budget?))
```

Let's write `_multiplicative_shave` robust:
```
def _multiplicative_shave(a_init, deadline, max_iter=10000, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_raw(a)
    stale = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        c = _auto_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M): break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc; best_a = a.copy()
        # target
        target = M * 0.999
        local_target = (best_sc / (2.0 * n)) * 0.999
        if local_target > target:
            target = local_target
        if target >= M:
            target = M * 0.999
        w = np.clip(c - target, 0.0)
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, M):
            tol = 0.002 * M
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            # choose top fractions
            thresh = M - tol
            w[c >= thresh] = 1.0
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum == 0:
                w[idx] = 1.0; wsum = 1.0
        w = w / wsum
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1]); wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0: break
        w = w / wsum
        h = _corr_a_A(a, w)
        hmean = float(np.mean(h))
        hmax = float(np.max(h)); hmin = float(np.min(h)); hspan = hmax - hmin
        if hspan < 1e-15:
            if rng is not None and (it % 25 == 0 or stale > 10):
                a = _normalize(a + 1e-4 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                continue
            stale += 1
            if stale > 30: break
            continue
        stale = 0
        coeffs = [0.2, 1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0]
        best_step = -1.0
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_sc = sc
        for coef in coeffs:
            step = coef / hspan
            zup = -step * (h - hmean)
            zup = np.clip(zup, -20.0, 20.0)
            at = a * np.exp(zup)
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            at += 1e-10 / n
            at = _normalize(at)
            ct = _auto_conv(at)
            ats = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
            if np.isfinite(ats) and ats < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                best_cand_sc = ats; best_cand = at; best_step = step
        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            sc = best_cand_sc
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc; best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            # tiny step
            step = 0.01 / hspan
            ... one more
            if none:
                if rng is not None:
                    a = _normalize(a + 1e-3*rng.standard_normal(n))
                stale += 1
                if stale > 40: break
    return best_a, best_sc
```
The `w = 0.5*(w+w[::-1])` for length odd, okay. For `w` length 2n-1 symmetric. Good.

Spectral newton:
```
def _spectral_newton(a_init, deadline, max_iter=50, weight_tol=0.01, K=None):
    a = _normalize(a_init); n=len(a)
    if K is None:
       if n <= 4096: K=40
       elif n <= 16384: K=30
       else: K=20
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_raw(a)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c=_auto_conv(a); M=float(c.max()); sc=float(2.0*n*M)
       if not finite: break
       if sc < best_sc: update
       beta_w = max(1e-15, weight_tol * M)
       shift=(c-M)/beta_w
       e=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-740,0))
       S=float(e.sum())
       if S < 1e-300:
           e=np.zeros_like(c); e[np.argmax(c)]=1; S=1
       w=e/S
       w = 0.5*(w+w[::-1]); w/=w.sum()
       h=_corr_a_A(a,w)
       g=h - np.mean(h)
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       d=_krylov_direction(g,w,K)
       dnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if dnorm < 1e-14:
           d=-g; d-=np.mean(d); dnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if dnorm < 1e-14: break
       # ensure descent relative to g (a-space gradient of weighted sum)
       if float(np.dot(d,g)) > -1e-14:
           d=-g; d-=np.mean(d)
           dnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
           if dnorm < 1e-14: break
       # cap
       if dnorm > 1.0:
           d *= 1.0/dnorm
       neg = d < -1e-14
       if neg.any():
           tmax = 0.99 * float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
       else:
           tmax = 1.0
       t = min(1.0, tmax)
       if t <= 0: break
       accepted=False
       for ls in range(40):
           at = a + t*d
           if np.min(at) < -1e-10:
               t *= 0.5
               if t <= 1e-12: break
               continue
           at = np.maximum(at,0.0); at = _normalize(at)
           ct=_auto_conv(at); ats=float(2.0*n*ct.max())
           if np.isfinite(ats) and (ats < sc - 1e-8 * t * max(1.0, gnorm) or (ls == 39 and ats < sc - 1e-13)):
               a=at; sc=ats; accepted=True; break
           t *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           d = -g; d -= np.mean(d); dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
           if dnorm < 1e-14: break
           if dnorm > 1.0: d *= 1.0/dnorm
           neg = d < -1e-14
           tmax = 0.99*min...; t=min(1,tmax)
           for ls in range(25): ...
           if not accepted: break
       if sc < best_sc: update
       # convergence if step small
       if dnorm < 1e-12:
           break
    return best_a,best_sc
```
`np.min(at)` on large array many times; okay. Could vector. If `t` becomes very small, break.
Acceptance tolerance: `1e-8 * t * max(1.0, gnorm)`. If gnorm=0.01, tol=1e-10. Good. If t small, tolerance smaller. Fine.

Potential problem: `g` is a-space gradient of weighted sum h (without factor 2). The line search uses actual max score, so descent condition maybe not aligned if weighted sum direction not reduce max immediately. But line search finds if actual max decreases. Fallback gradient.

`K` in `_krylov_direction`: If `K` > n, set `K=min(K, max(5, n//2))`. For n small.
Reorth in `_krylov_direction`: We need use loop over V; after `V.append(q)`, `Hp` orthogonalize against `V` including current q after alpha? We do `Hp = Hp - alpha*q`; then for l in range(len(V)-1): subtract previous (not current again). Good.
When initializing q, g norm. `V=[q]`. At j=0, len(V)-1=0 no previous. Beta. Append q. Then new q orthogonalize against all V including previous. Good.
Set `betas.append(beta)` for next offdiag. If break, betas length m-1. In T fill loop `for i in range(min(m-1, len(betas)))`. Good.
RHS: `gnorm * e0`.
If T eigenvalues all dropped, y zero, d zero -> fallback.

Maybe `_krylov_direction` returns a direction in a-space tangent. Good.

Now, final output. Need ensure no type boolean. Return list of float.
```

    n = a.size

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)
for non-negative sequences a.

The program uses:
- softmax/logit L-BFGS with adaptive log-sum-exp smoothing,
- multiplicative peak-shaving iterations,
- projected projected-Krylov/Newton-style polynomial polishing,
- multi-resolution upsampling (8192 -> 16384 -> 32768 -> optionally 65536),
- light diverse restarts.

All helper functions are top-level and do not use closures/lambdas.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Global state for scipy objective/gradient callbacks.
_OBJ_N = 0
_OBJ_BETA = 1.0
_OBJ_DEADLINE = 0.0
_KRYLOV_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _next_pow2(m):
    k = 1
    while k < m:
        k <<= 1
    if k < 2:
        k = 2
    return k


def _auto_conv(a):
    """Linear auto-convolution a*a, using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:out_len]
    return r


def _corr_a_A(a, A):
    """
    Return h_i = sum_p a[p] * A[i+p], i=0..n-1,
    where out-of-range A entries are treated as zero.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = A.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = n + m - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    fA = np.fft.rfft(A, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fA, N)[:out_len]
    return r[n - 1: n - 1 + n]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _score_raw(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _interpolate_to_n(a, n_new):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n_old = a.size
    n_new = int(max(1, n_new))
    if n_old == n_new:
        return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    a_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(a_new)


def _make_profile(n, kind, rng=None):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == "uniform":
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == "sin":
        a = np.sin(np.pi * t)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        return a / a.sum()
    if kind == "cos":
        if rng is not None:
            k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 4)))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        else:
            k = 1
            phase = 0.0
        a = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + phase)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        return a / a.sum()
    if kind == "asym":
        mu = 0.25
        sigma = 0.18
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - mu) / sigma) ** 2.0)
        a += 0.05
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        return a / a.sum()
    if kind == "power":
        a = (t + 1e-6) ** 2.0
        return a / a.sum()
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n


def _perturb_log(a, sigma, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = z + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    return _softmax(z)


def _perturb_fourier(a, rng, n_modes=5):
    a = _normalize(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    n = a.size
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(int(max(1, n_modes))):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 5)))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z = z + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t / n + phase)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return _softmax(z)


def _lsobj(z):
    if time.monotonic() > _OBJ_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("objective deadline")
    n = int(_OBJ_N)
    beta = float(_OBJ_BETA)
    if n <= 0 or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    beta = max(beta, 1e-15)

    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf

    shift = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        val = 2.0 * n * cmax
    else:
        val = 2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * float(np.log(s)))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 2.0 * n * cmax
    return float(val)


def _lsgrad(z):
    if time.monotonic() > _OBJ_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("gradient deadline")
    n = int(_OBJ_N)
    beta = float(_OBJ_BETA)
    if n <= 0 or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = max(beta, 1e-15)

    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shift = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s

    h = _corr_a_A(a, w)
    W = float(np.dot(w, c))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - W)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(grad)
    return grad


def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_remaining=30.0):
    global _OBJ_N, _OBJ_BETA, _OBJ_DEADLINE

    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    a_best = a.copy()
    sc_best = _score_raw(a_best)

    c = _auto_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return a_best, sc_best

    cs = np.sort(c)[::-1]
    if cs.size > 10:
        gap = float(cs[0] - cs[9])
    else:
        gap = float(cs[0])
    base_beta = max(gap, M * 0.02, M * 1e-6, 1e-15)

    if n <= 4096:
        factors = [1.0, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.16, 0.10, 0.06, 0.04, 0.025, 0.015,
                   0.009, 0.005, 0.003, 0.0018, 0.001, 0.0006, 0.00035,
                   0.0002, 0.00012, 0.00007, 0.00004]
    elif n <= 16384:
        factors = [1.0, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.16, 0.10, 0.06, 0.04, 0.025, 0.015,
                   0.009, 0.005, 0.003, 0.0018, 0.001, 0.0006, 0.00035, 0.0002]
    else:
        factors = [1.0, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.16, 0.10, 0.06, 0.04, 0.025, 0.015,
                   0.009, 0.005, 0.003, 0.0018, 0.001, 0.0006]

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a_best, 1e-30))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    # Rough per-iteration cost estimate, conservative for line-search evals.
    tpi = 0.03 + 0.000006 * float(n)

    for f in factors:
        if time.monotonic() + min_remaining > deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - min_remaining - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 10.0:
            break

        beta = base_beta * float(f)
        beta = max(beta, M * 1e-8, 1e-15)

        max_iter = int(min(6000, max(50, remaining / (2.0 * tpi))))
        gtol = 1e-10 if f < 0.02 else 1e-8

        _OBJ_N = n
        _OBJ_BETA = float(beta)
        _OBJ_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 1.0)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lsobj,
                z,
                jac=_lsgrad,
                method="L-BFGS",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max_iter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": gtol,
                    "maxcor": 50,
                    "maxls": 50,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
                    a_new = _softmax(x)
                    sc_new = _score_raw(a_new)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                        if sc_new < sc_best:
                            sc_best = float(sc_new)
                            a_best = a_new
                            z = np.log(np.maximum(a_best, 1e-30))
                            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                        elif sc_new < sc_best + 1e-10:
                            z = np.log(np.maximum(a_new, 1e-30))
                            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() + min_remaining > deadline:
            break

    return a_best, sc_best


def _multiplicative_shave(a_init, deadline, max_iter=10000, rng=None, target_score=None):
    global _KRYLOV_DEADLINE
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_raw(a)
    stale = 0

    for _ in range(int(max(0, max_iter))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _auto_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        local_target = (best_sc / (2.0 * n)) * 0.999
        target = M * 0.999
        if target_score is not None:
            ext_target = float(target_score) / (2.0 * n)
            if np.isfinite(ext_target) and ext_target < target:
                target = max(ext_target, M * 0.98)
        if local_target > target:
            target = local_target
        if target >= M:
            target = M * 0.999

        w = np.clip(c - target, 0.0)
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, M):
            thresh = M - 0.002 * M
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[c >= thresh] = 1.0
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum <= 0.0:
                w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                wsum = 1.0
        w = w / wsum
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / wsum

        h = _corr_a_A(a, w)
        hmean = float(np.mean(h))
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        hmin = float(np.min(h))
        hspan = hmax - hmin

        if hspan < 1e-15:
            if rng is not None and (stale > 5 or stale % 25 == 0):
                a = _normalize(a + 1e-4 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            stale += 1
            if stale > 35:
                break
            continue

        stale = 0
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_sc = sc
        coeff_list = [0.2, 1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0]

        for coef in coeff_list:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            step = float(coef) / hspan
            zup = -step * (h - hmean)
            zup = np.clip(zup, -20.0, 20.0)
            at = a * np.exp(zup)
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            at += 1e-10 / float(n)
            at = _normalize(at)
            ct = _auto_conv(at)
            atsc = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
            if np.isfinite(atsc) and atsc < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                best_cand_sc = atsc
                best_cand = at

        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            sc = best_cand_sc
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            # One very small perturbation.
            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                step = 0.01 / hspan
                zup = np.clip(-step * (h - hmean), -20.0, 20.0)
                at = a * np.exp(zup)
                at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
                at += 1e-10 / float(n)
                at = _normalize(at)
                ct = _auto_conv(at)
                atsc = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
                if np.isfinite(atsc) and atsc < sc:
                    a = at
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_sc = atsc
                else:
                    if rng is not None:
                        a = _normalize(a + 1e-3 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                    stale += 1
                    if stale > 45:
                        break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _hankel_hvp_proj(v, w):
    hv = _corr_a_A(v, w)
    hv -= float(np.mean(hv))
    return hv


def _krylov_direction(g, w, K=30):
    """
    Approximate Newton direction for a projected Hankel quadratic.
    Returns a descent direction in the tangent space (zero-sum).
    """
    global _KRYLOV_DEADLINE

    n = g.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if gnorm <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(min(K, max(5, n // 2)))
    V = []
    alphas = []
    betas = []
    q = g / gnorm

    for _ in range(K):
        if time.monotonic() > _KRYLOV_DEADLINE:
            break
        V.append(q)
        Hp = _hankel_hvp_proj(q, w)
        alpha = float(np.dot(q, Hp))
        alphas.append(alpha)
        Hp = Hp - alpha * q

        # Reorthogonalization for stability.
        for l in range(len(V) - 1):
            vl = V[l]
            Hp = Hp - float(np.dot(vl, Hp)) * vl

        beta = float(np.linalg.norm(Hp))
        if beta < 1e-12:
            break

        q = Hp / beta
        for l in range(len(V)):
            vl = V[l]
            q = q - float(np.dot(vl, q)) * vl
        qn = float(np.linalg.norm(q))
        if qn < 1e-12:
            break
        q = q / qn
        betas.append(beta)

    m = len(V)
    if m == 0:
        return -g

    T = np.diag(alphas[:m]).copy()
    for i in range(min(m - 1, len(betas))):
        T[i, i + 1] = betas[i]
        T[i + 1, i] = betas[i]

    rhs = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    rhs[0] = gnorm

    try:
        vals, vecs = np.linalg.eigh(T)
    except Exception:
        d = -g
        d -= float(np.mean(d))
        return d

    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(vals))) if m > 0 else 1.0
    tol = 1e-8 * max(1.0, scale)
    inv = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i, val in enumerate(vals):
        if abs(val) > tol:
            inv[i] = 1.0 / val
        else:
            inv[i] = 0.0

    altered_rhs = (vecs @ rhs) * inv
    y = vecs @ altered_rhs

    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i, v in enumerate(V):
        if y[i] != 0.0:
            u += float(y[i]) * v
    u -= float(np.mean(u))
    d = -u
    dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dnorm < 1e-14:
        d = -g
        d -= float(np.mean(d))
        return d

    if dnorm > 1.0:
        d *= 1.0 / dnorm

    # Ensure it is actually a descent direction for the current a-space gradient.
    if float(np.dot(d, g)) >= -1e-14 * gnorm * gnorm:
        d = -g
        d -= float(np.mean(d))
        dn2 = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dn2 > 1.0:
            d *= 1.0 / dn2

    return d


def _spectral_newton(a_init, deadline, max_iter=50, weight_tol=0.01, K=None, target_score=None):
    global _KRYLOV_DEADLINE

    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if K is None:
        if n <= 2048:
            K = 50
        elif n <= 8192:
            K = 40
        elif n <= 32768:
            K = 25
        else:
            K = 15

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_raw(a)

    for _ in range(int(max(0, max_iter))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _auto_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        # Weights: emphasize lags above the target if possible; otherwise near the maximum.
        w = None
        if target_score is not None:
            ext_peak = float(target_score) / (2.0 * n)
            if np.isfinite(ext_peak) and ext_peak < M * 0.99:
                w = np.clip(c - ext_peak, 0.0)
                wsum = float(w.sum())
                if wsum > 1e-12 * max(1.0, M):
                    w = w / wsum

        if w is None:
            beta_w = max(1e-15, float(weight_tol) * M)
            shift = (c - M) / beta_w
            e = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
            S = float(e.sum())
            if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
                e = np.zeros_like(c)
                e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                S = 1.0
            w = e / S

        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / wsum

        h = _corr_a_A(a, w)
        g = h - float(np.mean(h))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        _KRYLOV_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 0.5)
        d = _krylov_direction(g, w, K)
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dnorm < 1e-14:
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        if dnorm > 1.0:
            d *= 1.0 / dnorm

        if float(np.dot(d, g)) >= -1e-14 * gnorm * gnorm:
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if dnorm < 1e-14:
                break
            if dnorm > 1.0:
                d *= 1.0 / dnorm

        neg = d < -1e-14
        if neg.any():
            tmax = 0.99 * float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
        else:
            tmax = 1.0
        t = min(1.0, tmax)
        if t <= 0.0:
            break

        accepted = False
        for ls in range(40):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            at = a + t * d
            if np.min(at) < -1e-10:
                t *= 0.5
                if t <= 1e-12:
                    break
                continue
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            at = _normalize(at)
            ct = _auto_conv(at)
            atsc = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
            if np.isfinite(atsc):
                if atsc < sc - 1e-8 * t * max(1.0, gnorm) or (ls == 39 and atsc < sc - 1e-14):
                    a = at
                    sc = atsc
                    accepted = True
                    break
            t *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if dnorm < 1e-14:
                break
            if dnorm > 1.0:
                d *= 1.0 / dnorm
            neg = d < -1e-14
            if neg.any():
                tmax = 0.99 * float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
            else:
                tmax = 1.0
            t = min(1.0, tmax)
            if t <= 0.0:
                break
            for ls in range(25):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                at = a + t * d
                if np.min(at) < -1e-10:
                    t *= 0.5
                    if t <= 1e-12:
                        break
                    continue
                at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
                at = _normalize(at)
                ct = _auto_conv(at)
                atsc = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
                if np.isfinite(atsc) and (atsc < sc - 1e-10 * t * max(1.0, gnorm) or (ls == 24 and atsc < sc - 1e-14)):
                    a = at
                    sc = atsc
                    accepted = True
                    break
                t *= 0.5
            if not accepted:
                break

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if dnorm < 1e-12:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _optimize_size_phases(a_init, size_deadline, target_score, rng):
    a_best = _normalize(a_init)
    sc_best = _score_raw(a_best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = size_deadline - t0
    if budget < 60.0:
        return a_best, sc_best

    # Phase A: L-BFGS smoothed max.
    lb_end = min(size_deadline - max(60.0, 0.42 * budget), t0 + 0.52 * budget)
    if lb_end - time.monotonic() > 25.0:
        a_lb, sc_lb = _run_lbfgs(a_best, lb_end, min_remaining=10.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc_lb) and sc_lb < sc_best:
            a_best, sc_best = a_lb, sc_lb

    # Phase B: multiplicative peak shaving.
    mp_budget = 0.20 * budget
    mp_end = min(size_deadline - max(40.0, 0.30 * budget), time.monotonic() + mp_budget)
    if mp_end - time.monotonic() > 15.0:
        a_mp, sc_mp = _multiplicative_shave(
            a_best, mp_end, rng=rng, target_score=target_score
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp < sc_best:
            a_best, sc_best = a_mp, sc_mp

    # Phase C: projected Newton/Krylov polishing.
    sp_budget = 0.22 * budget
    sp_end = min(size_deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + sp_budget)
    if sp_end - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        a_sp, sc_sp = _spectral_newton(
            a_best, sp_end, target_score=target_score
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_sp) and sc_sp < sc_best:
            a_best, sc_best = a_sp, sc_sp

    # Phase D: short diverse restarts using multiplicative search.
    if time.monotonic() < size_deadline - 15.0:
        for sigma in (0.04, 0.10):
            if time.monotonic() > size_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            p = _perturb_log(a_best, sigma, rng)
            a_d, sc_d = _multiplicative_shave(
                p,
                min(size_deadline, time.monotonic() + 25.0),
                rng=rng,
                target_score=target_score,
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_d) and sc_d < sc_best:
                a_best, sc_best = a_d, sc_d

    return a_best, sc_best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    # Load parent sequence if present.
    parent = None
    n_base = 0
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_raw.size > 0:
            parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(parent_raw.sum())
            if s > 0.01:
                parent = parent_raw / s
                n_base = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n_base = 4096
        parent = _make_profile(n_base, "uniform", rng)

    best_a = _normalize(parent)
    best_sc = _score_raw(best_a)
    print(f"[init] n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Multi-resolution sizes up to 32768.
    max_base = 32768
    targets = []
    cur = max(256, int(n_base))
    while len(targets) < 4 and cur <= max_base:
        targets.append(int(cur))
        if cur >= max_base:
            break
        cur *= 2

    # De-duplicate while preserving order.
    uniq_targets = []
    for x in targets:
        if x not in uniq_targets:
            uniq_targets.append(x)
    targets = uniq_targets

    # Reserve time for optional extra size and final polish.
    if n_base >= 8192:
        extra_reserve = 500.0
    elif n_base >= 4096:
        extra_reserve = 300.0
    else:
        extra_reserve = 200.0

    final_reserve = 300.0
    main_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + max(600.0, (deadline - t_start) - final_reserve - extra_reserve))
    total_alloc = max(300.0, main_deadline - t_start)
    weights = [float(i + 1) for i in range(len(targets))]
    sum_w = float(sum(weights))

    goal_score = 1.5026
    last_size_improved = False

    for idx, n_target in enumerate(targets):
        t_now = time.monotonic()
        if t_now > main_deadline - 100.0:
            break

        alloc = total_alloc * (weights[idx] / sum_w)
        if alloc > main_deadline - t_now - 80.0:
            alloc = max(80.0, main_deadline - t_now - 80.0)
        if alloc < 80.0:
            continue

        size_deadline = min(main_deadline - 20.0, t_now + alloc)
        aim_score = goal_score if best_sc > goal_score else best_sc * 0.999

        a_init = _interpolate_to_n(best_a, n_target)
        a_new, sc_new = _optimize_size_phases(
            a_init,
            size_deadline,
            target_score=float(aim_score),
            rng=rng,
        )

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            last_size_improved = True
            print(f"[size {n_target}] NEW BEST score={best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            last_size_improved = False
            print(f"[size {n_target}] best unchanged score={best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Optional 65536 if 32768 helped and time remains.
    if last_size_improved and len(targets) > 0 and targets[-1] < 65536 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 900.0:
        n_extra = min(65536, targets[-1] * 2)
        extra_end = min(deadline - final_reserve, time.monotonic() + extra_reserve)
        if extra_end - time.monotonic() > 300.0:
            aim_score = goal_score if best_sc > goal_score else best_sc * 0.999
            a_init = _interpolate_to_n(best_a, int(n_extra))
            a_new, sc_new = _optimize_size_phases(
                a_init,
                extra_end,
                target_score=float(aim_score),
                rng=rng,
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc_new)
                print(f"[extra {n_extra}] NEW BEST score={best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Final polish on current best.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        aim_score = goal_score if best_sc > goal_score else best_sc * 0.999
        a_new, sc_new = _optimize_size_phases(
            best_a,
            min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())),
            target_score=float(aim_score),
            rng=rng,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            print(f"[final] NEW BEST score={best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Final safety normalization and validation.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(best_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        best_a = _make_profile(256, "uniform")

    # Optional final evaluation (the evaluator is assumed to be in scope).
    try:
        _ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
        print(f"[validate] evaluate_sequence={float(_ev):.10f}, internal={_score_raw(best_a):.10f}, n={best_a.size}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
